      "\"\"\"",
      "==============================================",
      "LAB TASK 02: CONNECT FOUR (Adversarial Search)",
      "==============================================",
      "",
      "Algorithm: Minimax with Alpha-Beta Pruning + Depth Limit",
      "Board: 6 rows x 7 columns",
      "AI = X (MAX), Human = O (MIN)",
      "",
      "Rules: Drop disc in column, falls to lowest empty row.",
      "        Connect 4 horizontally, vertically, or diagonally.",
      "\"\"\"",
      "import math",
      "import random",
      "",
      "class ConnectFour:",
      "    ROWS = 6",
      "    COLS = 7",
      "    ",
      "    def __init__(self):",
      "        self.board = [[' ' for _ in range(self.COLS)] for _ in range(self.ROWS)]",
      "        self.current_player = 'X'  # AI goes first",
      "    ",
      "    def print_board(self):",
      "        \"\"\"Display the board.\"\"\"",
      "        print()",
      "        for row in self.board:",
      "            print('|', end='')",
      "            for cell in row:",
      "                print(f' {cell} ', end='|')",
      "            print()",
      "        print('+' + '---+' * self.COLS)",
      "        print(' ', end='')",
      "        for col in range(self.COLS):",
      "            print(f' {col}  ', end='')",
      "        print()",
      "    ",
      "    def is_valid_move(self, col):",
      "        \"\"\"Check if column has space.\"\"\"",
      "        return self.board[0][col] == ' '",
      "    ",
      "    def get_next_open_row(self, col):",
      "        \"\"\"Find lowest empty row in column.\"\"\"",
      "        for row in range(self.ROWS - 1, -1, -1):",
      "            if self.board[row][col] == ' ':",
      "                return row",
      "        return None",
      "    ",
      "    def drop_disc(self, col, player):",
      "        \"\"\"Place disc in column.\"\"\"",
      "        row = self.get_next_open_row(col)",
      "        if row is not None:",
      "            self.board[row][col] = player",
      "            return row",
      "        return None",
      "    ",
      "    def remove_disc(self, col):",
      "        \"\"\"Remove top disc from column.\"\"\"",
      "        for row in range(self.ROWS):",
      "            if self.board[row][col] != ' ':",
      "                self.board[row][col] = ' '",
      "                break",
      "    ",
      "    def check_winner(self, player):",
      "        \"\"\"Check 4-in-a-row in all directions.\"\"\"",
      "        # Horizontal",
      "        for row in range(self.ROWS):",
      "            for col in range(self.COLS - 3):",
      "                if all(self.board[row][col+i] == player for i in range(4)):",
      "                    return True",
      "        # Vertical",
      "        for row in range(self.ROWS - 3):",
      "            for col in range(self.COLS):",
      "                if all(self.board[row+i][col] == player for i in range(4)):",
      "                    return True",
      "        # Diagonal (positive slope)",
      "        for row in range(3, self.ROWS):",
      "            for col in range(self.COLS - 3):",
      "                if all(self.board[row-i][col+i] == player for i in range(4)):",
      "                    return True",
      "        # Diagonal (negative slope)",
      "        for row in range(self.ROWS - 3):",
      "            for col in range(self.COLS - 3):",
      "                if all(self.board[row+i][col+i] == player for i in range(4)):",
      "                    return True",
      "        return False",
      "    ",
      "    def is_draw(self):",
      "        \"\"\"Check if board is full.\"\"\"",
      "        return all(cell != ' ' for row in self.board for cell in row)",
      "    ",
      "    def evaluate_window(self, window, player):",
      "        \"\"\"Score a window of 4 cells.\"\"\"",
      "        score = 0",
      "        opp = 'O' if player == 'X' else 'X'",
      "        ",
      "        if window.count(player) == 4: score += 100",
      "        elif window.count(player) == 3 and window.count(' ') == 1: score += 5",
      "        elif window.count(player) == 2 and window.count(' ') == 2: score += 2",
      "        ",
      "        if window.count(opp) == 3 and window.count(' ') == 1: score -= 4",
      "        ",
      "        return score",
      "    ",
      "    def evaluate_board(self):",
      "        \"\"\"Heuristic evaluation of board state.\"\"\"",
      "        score = 0",
      "        center_array = [self.board[i][3] for i in range(self.ROWS)]",
      "        center_count = center_array.count('X')",
      "        score += center_count * 3",
      "        ",
      "        # Score horizontal",
      "        for row in range(self.ROWS):",
      "            for col in range(self.COLS - 3):",
      "                window = [self.board[row][col+i] for i in range(4)]",
      "                score += self.evaluate_window(window, 'X')",
      "        # Score vertical",
      "        for col in range(self.COLS):",
      "            for row in range(self.ROWS - 3):",
      "                window = [self.board[row+i][col] for i in range(4)]",
      "                score += self.evaluate_window(window, 'X')",
      "        # Score diagonals",
      "        for row in range(3, self.ROWS):",
      "            for col in range(self.COLS - 3):",
      "                window = [self.board[row-i][col+i] for i in range(4)]",
      "                score += self.evaluate_window(window, 'X')",
      "        for row in range(self.ROWS - 3):",
      "            for col in range(self.COLS - 3):",
      "                window = [self.board[row+i][col+i] for i in range(4)]",
      "                score += self.evaluate_window(window, 'X')",
      "        return score",
      "    ",
      "    def minimax(self, depth, alpha, beta, is_maximizing):",
      "        \"\"\"Minimax with Alpha-Beta + depth limit.\"\"\"",
      "        valid_cols = [c for c in range(self.COLS) if self.is_valid_move(c)]",
      "        ",
      "        if self.check_winner('X'): return 1000 + depth",
      "        if self.check_winner('O'): return -1000 - depth",
      "        if self.is_draw() or depth == 0: return self.evaluate_board()",
      "        ",
      "        if is_maximizing:",
      "            best = -math.inf",
      "            for col in valid_cols:",
      "                self.drop_disc(col, 'X')",
      "                best = max(best, self.minimax(depth-1, alpha, beta, False))",
      "                self.remove_disc(col)",
      "                alpha = max(alpha, best)",
      "                if beta <= alpha: break",
      "            return best",
      "        else:",
      "            best = math.inf",
      "            for col in valid_cols:",
      "                self.drop_disc(col, 'O')",
      "                best = min(best, self.minimax(depth-1, alpha, beta, True))",
      "                self.remove_disc(col)",
      "                beta = min(beta, best)",
      "                if beta <= alpha: break",
      "            return best",
      "    ",
      "    def get_best_move(self, depth=5):",
      "        \"\"\"Find best column for AI.\"\"\"",
      "        best_score = -math.inf",
      "        best_col = random.choice([c for c in range(self.COLS) if self.is_valid_move(c)])",
      "        alpha, beta = -math.inf, math.inf",
      "        ",
      "        for col in range(self.COLS):",
      "            if self.is_valid_move(col):",
      "                self.drop_disc(col, 'X')",
      "                score = self.minimax(depth-1, alpha, beta, False)",
      "                self.remove_disc(col)",
      "                if score > best_score:",
      "                    best_score = score",
      "                    best_col = col",
      "        return best_col",
      "    ",
      "    def play(self):",
      "        print('=' * 50)",
      "        print('🎮 CONNECT FOUR: You (O) vs AI (X)')",
      "        print('=' * 50)",
      "        while True:",
      "            self.print_board()",
      "            if self.current_player == 'X':",
      "                print('AI (X) is thinking...')",
      "                col = self.get_best_move(depth=4)",
      "                self.drop_disc(col, 'X')",
      "                print(f'AI dropped in column {col}')",
      "                if self.check_winner('X'):",
      "                    self.print_board()",
      "                    print('🏆 AI wins!')",
      "                    break",
      "            else:",
      "                try:",
      "                    col = int(input('Your turn (O). Choose column (0-6): '))",
      "                    if col < 0 or col >= self.COLS or not self.is_valid_move(col):",
      "                        print('❌ Invalid column!')",
      "                        continue",
      "                    self.drop_disc(col, 'O')",
      "                except ValueError:",
      "                    print('❌ Enter a number!')",
      "                    continue",
      "                if self.check_winner('O'):",
      "                    self.print_board()",
      "                    print('🎉 You win!')",
      "                    break",
      "            if self.is_draw():",
      "                self.print_board()",
      "                print('🤝 Draw!')",
      "                break",
      "            self.current_player = 'O' if self.current_player == 'X' else 'X'",
      "",
      "if __name__ == '__main__':",
      "    game = ConnectFour()",
      "    game.play()",
      "$0"

  "Adversarial Search - Nim Game": {
    "prefix": "ai_nim",
    "body": [
      "\"\"\"",
      "==============================================",
      "LAB TASK 03: NIM GAME (Adversarial Search)",
      "==============================================",
      "",
      "Algorithm: Minimax with Alpha-Beta Pruning",
      "Rules: Start with N objects. Each turn remove 1, 2, or 3.",
      "       Player who takes the LAST object WINS.",
      "AI = MAX, Human = MIN",
      "\"\"\"",
      "import math",
      "",
      "class NimGame:",
      "    def __init__(self, initial_pile=12):",
      "        self.pile = initial_pile",
      "        self.initial_pile = initial_pile",
      "        self.current_player = 'AI'  # AI goes first",
      "    ",
      "    def is_terminal(self):",
      "        \"\"\"Game ends when pile is empty.\"\"\"",
      "        return self.pile == 0",
      "    ",
      "    def get_winner(self):",
      "        \"\"\"Last player to move wins.\"\"\"",
      "        if self.current_player == 'AI':",
      "            return 'Human'  # AI just moved and won",
      "        else:",
      "            return 'AI'  # Human just moved and won? Actually opposite",
      "    ",
      "    def evaluate(self, is_max_turn):",
      "        \"\"\"",
      "        Evaluate position.",
      "        Terminal: +1 AI wins, -1 Human wins.",
      "        Non-terminal: use mathematical property.",
      "        Nim strategy: Winning positions are multiples of 4.",
      "        \"\"\"",
      "        if self.pile == 0:",
      "            return -1 if is_max_turn else 1",
      "        # Heuristic: multiples of 4 are losing for current player",
      "        if self.pile % 4 == 0:",
      "            return -0.5  # Bad for player to move",
      "        else:",
      "            return 0.5   # Good for player to move",
      "    ",
      "    def minimax(self, depth, is_maximizing, alpha, beta):",
      "        \"\"\"Minimax with Alpha-Beta Pruning.\"\"\"",
      "        if self.pile == 0:",
      "            return -1 if is_maximizing else 1",
      "        ",
      "        if depth == 0:",
      "            return self.evaluate(is_maximizing)",
      "        ",
      "        if is_maximizing:  # AI's turn",
      "            best = -math.inf",
      "            for remove in range(1, min(4, self.pile + 1)):",
      "                self.pile -= remove",
      "                best = max(best, self.minimax(depth-1, False, alpha, beta))",
      "                self.pile += remove",
      "                alpha = max(alpha, best)",
      "                if beta <= alpha:",
      "                    break",
      "            return best",
      "        else:  # Human's turn",
      "            best = math.inf",
      "            for remove in range(1, min(4, self.pile + 1)):",
      "                self.pile -= remove",
      "                best = min(best, self.minimax(depth-1, True, alpha, beta))",
      "                self.pile += remove",
      "                beta = min(beta, best)",
      "                if beta <= alpha:",
      "                    break",
      "            return best",
      "    ",
      "    def get_best_move(self):",
      "        \"\"\"Find optimal number to remove.\"\"\"",
      "        # Optimal Nim strategy: keep pile as multiple of 4",
      "        best_score = -math.inf",
      "        best_remove = 1",
      "        alpha, beta = -math.inf, math.inf",
      "        ",
      "        for remove in range(1, min(4, self.pile + 1)):",
      "            self.pile -= remove",
      "            score = self.minimax(10, False, alpha, beta)",
      "            self.pile += remove",
      "            if score > best_score:",
      "                best_score = score",
      "                best_remove = remove",
      "        return best_remove",
      "    ",
      "    def play(self):",
      "        print('=' * 40)",
      "        print(f'🎮 NIM GAME: Starting pile = {self.pile}')",
      "        print('  Rules: Remove 1, 2, or 3 objects.')",
      "        print('  Player who takes the LAST object WINS!')",
      "        print('=' * 40)",
      "        while self.pile > 0:",
      "            print(f'\\nCurrent pile: {self.pile} objects')",
      "            if self.current_player == 'AI':",
      "                remove = self.get_best_move()",
      "                self.pile -= remove",
      "                print(f'AI removes {remove} object(s). Remaining: {self.pile}')",
      "                if self.pile == 0:",
      "                    print('🏆 AI wins!')",
      "                    break",
      "                self.current_player = 'Human'",
      "            else:",
      "                try:",
      "                    remove = int(input(f'Your turn. Remove (1-{min(3, self.pile)}): '))",
      "                    if remove < 1 or remove > min(3, self.pile):",
      "                        print(f'❌ Remove between 1 and {min(3, self.pile)}!')",
      "                        continue",
      "                    self.pile -= remove",
      "                    if self.pile == 0:",
      "                        print('🎉 You win!')",
      "                        break",
      "                    self.current_player = 'AI'",
      "                except ValueError:",
      "                    print('❌ Enter a number!')",
      "",
      "if __name__ == '__main__':",
      "    pile_size = int(input('Enter starting pile size (e.g., 12): ') or 12)",
      "    game = NimGame(pile_size)",
      "    game.play()",
      "$0"
    ],
    "description": "Nim Game with Minimax + Alpha-Beta + Mathematical Optimal Strategy"

  "Adversarial Search - Mancala": {
    "prefix": "ai_mancala",
    "body": [
      "\"\"\"",
      "==============================================",
      "LAB TASK 04: MANCALA (Adversarial Search)",
      "==============================================",
      "",
      "Algorithm: Minimax with Alpha-Beta Pruning + Depth Limit",
      "Board: 6 pits per side + 1 Mancala each",
      "AI = Player 2 (Bottom), Human = Player 1 (Top)",
      "\"\"\"",
      "import math",
      "import random",
      "",
      "class Mancala:",
      "    PITS_PER_SIDE = 6",
      "    INITIAL_STONES = 4",
      "    ",
      "    def __init__(self):",
      "        # Board layout:",
      "        # pits[0:6] = Player 1's pits (top row, left to right)",
      "        # pit[6] = Player 1's Mancala",
      "        # pits[7:13] = Player 2's pits (bottom row, left to right)",
      "        # pit[13] = Player 2's Mancala",
      "        self.pits = [self.INITIAL_STONES] * 6 + [0] + \\",
      "                     [self.INITIAL_STONES] * 6 + [0]",
      "        self.current_player = 2  # AI (Player 2) goes first",
      "    ",
      "    def print_board(self):",
      "        \"\"\"Display the Mancala board.\"\"\"",
      "        print()",
      "        # Player 1's side (top)",
      "        print(f'    ', end='')",
      "        for i in range(5, -1, -1):",
      "            print(f'{self.pits[i]:2} ', end='')",
      "        print()",
      "        # Mancalas",
      "        print(f'P1({self.pits[6]:2})', end='')",
      "        print(' ' * 15, end='')",
      "        print(f'P2({self.pits[13]:2})')",
      "        # Player 2's side (bottom)",
      "        print(f'    ', end='')",
      "        for i in range(7, 13):",
      "            print(f'{self.pits[i]:2} ', end='')",
      "        print()",
      "        print()",
      "    ",
      "    def is_game_over(self):",
      "        \"\"\"Game ends when one side is empty.\"\"\"",
      "        return (sum(self.pits[0:6]) == 0 or sum(self.pits[7:13]) == 0)",
      "    ",
      "    def get_winner(self):",
      "        \"\"\"Determine winner based on Mancala counts.\"\"\"",
      "        p1_score = self.pits[6]",
      "        p2_score = self.pits[13]",
      "        if p1_score > p2_score: return 1",
      "        elif p2_score > p1_score: return 2",
      "        else: return 0  # Draw",
      "    ",
      "    def get_valid_moves(self, player):",
      "        \"\"\"Return list of valid pit indices for player.\"\"\"",
      "        if player == 1:",
      "            return [i for i in range(6) if self.pits[i] > 0]",
      "        else:",
      "            return [i for i in range(7, 13) if self.pits[i] > 0]",
      "    ",
      "    def make_move(self, pit_idx):",
      "        \"\"\"",
      "        Execute a move. Returns True if extra turn earned.",
      "        \"\"\"",
      "        stones = self.pits[pit_idx]",
      "        self.pits[pit_idx] = 0",
      "        current = pit_idx",
      "        ",
      "        while stones > 0:",
      "            current = (current + 1) % 14",
      "            # Skip opponent's Mancala",
      "            if self.current_player == 1 and current == 13:",
      "                continue",
      "            if self.current_player == 2 and current == 6:",
      "                continue",
      "            self.pits[current] += 1",
      "            stones -= 1",
      "        ",
      "        # Capture rule",
      "        if self.current_player == 1 and 0 <= current <= 5:",
      "            if self.pits[current] == 1 and self.pits[12 - current] > 0:",
      "                self.pits[6] += self.pits[current] + self.pits[12 - current]",
      "                self.pits[current] = 0",
      "                self.pits[12 - current] = 0",
      "        elif self.current_player == 2 and 7 <= current <= 12:",
      "            if self.pits[current] == 1 and self.pits[12 - current] > 0:",
      "                self.pits[13] += self.pits[current] + self.pits[12 - current]",
      "                self.pits[current] = 0",
      "                self.pits[12 - current] = 0",
      "        ",
      "        # Extra turn if last stone lands in own Mancala",
      "        if self.current_player == 1 and current == 6:",
      "            return True",
      "        if self.current_player == 2 and current == 13:",
      "            return True",
      "        return False",
      "    ",
      "    def evaluate(self):",
      "        \"\"\"Heuristic: difference in Mancala stores.\"\"\"",
      "        return self.pits[13] - self.pits[6]",
      "    ",
      "    def minimax(self, depth, is_maximizing, alpha, beta):",
      "        \"\"\"Minimax with Alpha-Beta.\"\"\"",
      "        if self.is_game_over() or depth == 0:",
      "            return self.evaluate()",
      "        ",
      "        if is_maximizing:  # AI (Player 2)",
      "            best = -math.inf",
      "            moves = self.get_valid_moves(2)",
      "            if not moves: return self.evaluate()",
      "            for move in moves:",
      "                backup = self.pits[:]",
      "                extra_turn = self.make_move(move)",
      "                if extra_turn:",
      "                    score = self.minimax(depth-1, True, alpha, beta)",
      "                else:",
      "                    self.current_player = 1",
      "                    score = self.minimax(depth-1, False, alpha, beta)",
      "                    self.current_player = 2",
      "                self.pits = backup",
      "                best = max(best, score)",
      "                alpha = max(alpha, best)",
      "                if beta <= alpha: break",
      "            return best",
      "        else:  # Human (Player 1)",
      "            best = math.inf",
      "            moves = self.get_valid_moves(1)",
      "            if not moves: return self.evaluate()",
      "            for move in moves:",
      "                backup = self.pits[:]",
      "                self.current_player = 1",
      "                extra_turn = self.make_move(move)",
      "                if extra_turn:",
      "                    score = self.minimax(depth-1, False, alpha, beta)",
      "                else:",
      "                    self.current_player = 2",
      "                    score = self.minimax(depth-1, True, alpha, beta)",
      "                    self.current_player = 1",
      "                self.pits = backup",
      "                best = min(best, score)",
      "                beta = min(beta, best)",
      "                if beta <= alpha: break",
      "            return best",
      "    ",
      "    def get_best_move(self, depth=6):",
      "        \"\"\"Find best pit for AI.\"\"\"",
      "        best_score = -math.inf",
      "        moves = self.get_valid_moves(2)",
      "        best_move = random.choice(moves) if moves else None",
      "        alpha, beta = -math.inf, math.inf",
      "        ",
      "        for move in moves:",
      "            backup = self.pits[:]",
      "            extra_turn = self.make_move(move)",
      "            score = self.minimax(depth-1, extra_turn, alpha, beta)",
      "            self.pits = backup",
      "            if score > best_score:",
      "                best_score = score",
      "                best_move = move",
      "        return best_move",
      "    ",
      "    def play(self):",
      "        print('=' * 50)",
      "        print('🎮 MANCALA: You (P1) vs AI (P2)')",
      "        print('=' * 50)",
      "        ",
      "        while not self.is_game_over():",
      "            self.print_board()",
      "            if self.current_player == 2:  # AI",
      "                print('AI (Player 2) is thinking...')",
      "                move = self.get_best_move(depth=4)",
      "                extra = self.make_move(move)",
      "                print(f'AI played pit {move-6}')",
      "                if not extra:",
      "                    self.current_player = 1",
      "                else:",
      "                    print('AI gets an extra turn!')",
      "            else:  # Human",
      "                moves = self.get_valid_moves(1)",
      "                if not moves: break",
      "                try:",
      "                    pit = int(input(f'Your turn. Choose pit (1-6): '))",
      "                    move_idx = pit - 1",
      "                    if move_idx not in moves:",
      "                        print('❌ Invalid! Empty or out of range.')",
      "                        continue",
      "                    extra = self.make_move(move_idx)",
      "                    if not extra:",
      "                        self.current_player = 2",
      "                    else:",
      "                        print('You get an extra turn!')",
      "                except ValueError:",
      "                    print('❌ Enter a number 1-6!')",
      "        ",
      "        self.print_board()",
      "        winner = self.get_winner()",
      "        if winner == 1: print('🎉 You win!')",
      "        elif winner == 2: print('🏆 AI wins!')",
      "        else: print('🤝 Draw!')",
      "",
      "if __name__ == '__main__':",
      "    game = Mancala()",
      "    game.play()",
      "$0"
    ],
    "description": "Mancala with Minimax + Alpha-Beta Pruning + Capture & Extra Turn Rules"
  },

      "\"\"\"",
      "==============================================",
      "Q3 PART 2: CUSTOMER CLUSTERING WITH K-MEANS",
      "Using Aggregated Customer-Level Features",
      "==============================================",
      "\"\"\"",
      "import pandas as pd",
      "import numpy as np",
      "import matplotlib.pyplot as plt",
      "import seaborn as sns",
      "from sklearn.model_selection import train_test_split",
      "from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler",
      "from sklearn.cluster import KMeans",
      "from sklearn.decomposition import PCA",
      "from sklearn.metrics import silhouette_score",
      "import warnings",
      "warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')",
      "",
      "# =============================================",
      "# ASSUMPTION: df from Part 1 is loaded & cleaned",
      "# =============================================",
      "# If starting fresh, uncomment:",
      "# df = pd.read_csv('online_retail.csv', encoding='latin1')",
      "# df = df.dropna(subset=['CustomerID'])",
      "# df['Description'] = df['Description'].fillna('Unknown Product')",
      "# df = df[df['Quantity'] > 0]",
      "# df = df[df['UnitPrice'] > 0]",
      "# df['InvoiceDate'] = pd.to_datetime(df['InvoiceDate'])",
      "# df['TotalPrice'] = df['Quantity'] * df['UnitPrice']",
      "",
      "",
      "# =============================================",
      "# STEP 1: Create Customer-Level Aggregated Dataset",
      "# =============================================",
      "print('='*60)",
      "print('STEP 1: AGGREGATING CUSTOMER-LEVEL FEATURES')",
      "print('='*60)",
      "",
      "# Reference date for Recency calculation",
      "reference_date = df['InvoiceDate'].max() + pd.Timedelta(days=1)",
      "",
      "customer_df = df.groupby('CustomerID').agg(",
      "    TotalSpend=('TotalPrice', 'sum'),           # Monetary",
      "    PurchaseFrequency=('InvoiceNo', 'nunique'),  # Frequency",
      "    TotalQuantity=('Quantity', 'sum'),           # Total items bought",
      "    AvgBasketSize=('TotalPrice', 'mean'),        # Avg spend per transaction",
      "    LastPurchaseDate=('InvoiceDate', 'max')      # For Recency",
      ").reset_index()",
      "",
      "# Calculate Recency (days since last purchase)",
      "customer_df['Recency'] = (reference_date - customer_df['LastPurchaseDate']).dt.days",
      "",
      "# Drop the LastPurchaseDate as it's now encoded in Recency",
      "customer_df = customer_df.drop('LastPurchaseDate', axis=1)",
      "",
      "print(f'\\nCustomer-level dataset shape: {customer_df.shape}')",
      "print(f'\\nSample:')",
      "print(customer_df.head(10))",
      "print(f'\\nSummary Statistics:')",
      "print(customer_df.describe())",
      "",
      "",
      "# =============================================",
      "# STEP 2: Train-Test Split (80-20)",
      "# =============================================",
      "print('\\n' + '='*60)",
      "print('STEP 2: TRAIN-TEST SPLIT (80:20)')",
      "print('='*60)",
      "",
      "# Extract features for clustering (exclude CustomerID)",
      "feature_cols = ['TotalSpend', 'PurchaseFrequency', 'TotalQuantity', 'AvgBasketSize', 'Recency']",
      "X = customer_df[feature_cols].copy()",
      "",
      "X_train, X_test = train_test_split(X, test_size=0.2, random_state=42, shuffle=True)",
      "",
      "print(f'Training set size: {X_train.shape}')",
      "print(f'Testing set size: {X_test.shape}')",
      "",
      "",
      "# =============================================",
      "# STEP 3: Feature Scaling (Standardization)",
      "# =============================================",
      "print('\\n' + '='*60)",
      "print('STEP 3: STANDARDIZATION (Z-score Normalization)')",
      "print('='*60)",
      "",
      "scaler = StandardScaler()",
      "",
      "# Fit on training data, transform both sets",
      "X_train_scaled = scaler.fit_transform(X_train)",
      "X_test_scaled = scaler.transform(X_test)",
      "",
      "# Convert back to DataFrame for interpretability",
      "X_train_scaled = pd.DataFrame(X_train_scaled, columns=feature_cols, index=X_train.index)",
      "X_test_scaled = pd.DataFrame(X_test_scaled, columns=feature_cols, index=X_test.index)",
      "",
      "print(f'Training data after scaling (mean≈0, std≈1):')",
      "print(X_train_scaled.describe().round(2))",
      "",
      "",
      "# =============================================",
      "# STEP 4 & 5: K-Means + Elbow Method",
      "# =============================================",
      "print('\\n' + '='*60)",
      "print('STEP 4 & 5: ELBOW METHOD FOR OPTIMAL k')",
      "print('='*60)",
      "",
      "inertia = []",
      "silhouette_scores = []",
      "K_range = range(2, 11)",
      "",
      "for k in K_range:",
      "    kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=k, random_state=42, n_init=10)",
      "    kmeans.fit(X_train_scaled)",
      "    inertia.append(kmeans.inertia_)",
      "    ",
      "    # Silhouette score",
      "    labels = kmeans.labels_",
      "    sil_score = silhouette_score(X_train_scaled, labels)",
      "    silhouette_scores.append(sil_score)",
      "    print(f'k={k}: Inertia={kmeans.inertia_:.2f}, Silhouette={sil_score:.4f}')",
      "",
      "# Visualize Elbow + Silhouette",
      "fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(14, 5))",
      "",
      "axes[0].plot(K_range, inertia, marker='o', color='steelblue', linewidth=2)",
      "axes[0].set_title('Elbow Method (Inertia)', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')",
      "axes[0].set_xlabel('Number of Clusters (k)')",
      "axes[0].set_ylabel('Inertia')",
      "axes[0].grid(True, alpha=0.3)",
      "",
      "axes[1].plot(K_range, silhouette_scores, marker='s', color='coral', linewidth=2)",
      "axes[1].set_title('Silhouette Score', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')",
      "axes[1].set_xlabel('Number of Clusters (k)')",
      "axes[1].set_ylabel('Silhouette Score')",
      "axes[1].grid(True, alpha=0.3)",
      "",
      "plt.tight_layout()",
      "plt.show()",
      "",
      "# Select optimal k (based on elbow/silhouette)",
      "optimal_k = silhouette_scores.index(max(silhouette_scores)) + 2  # +2 because range starts at 2",
      "print(f'\\n🎯 Optimal number of clusters: k={optimal_k}')",
      "print(f'   Best Silhouette Score: {max(silhouette_scores):.4f}')",
      "",
      "",
      "# =============================================",
      "# STEP 6: Assign Cluster Labels & Store Centroids (Training)",
      "# =============================================",
      "print('\\n' + '='*60)",
      "print('STEP 6: ASSIGN CLUSTER LABELS & STORE CENTROIDS')",
      "print('='*60)",
      "",
      "# Train final K-Means with optimal k",
      "kmeans_final = KMeans(n_clusters=optimal_k, random_state=42, n_init=10)",
      "train_labels = kmeans_final.fit_predict(X_train_scaled)",
      "",
      "# Store centroids",
      "centroids = kmeans_final.cluster_centers_",
      "print(f'\\nCluster Centroids (in standardized scale):')",
      "centroid_df = pd.DataFrame(centroids, columns=feature_cols)",
      "print(centroid_df.round(3))",
      "",
      "# Add labels to training data",
      "X_train_labeled = X_train.copy()",
      "X_train_labeled['Cluster'] = train_labels",
      "",
      "print(f'\\nTraining samples per cluster:')",
      "print(X_train_labeled['Cluster'].value_counts().sort_index())",
      "",
      "",
      "# =============================================",
      "# STEP 7: Predict Cluster Labels for Test Data",
      "# =============================================",
      "print('\\n' + '='*60)",
      "print('STEP 7: PREDICT CLUSTERS FOR TEST DATA')",
      "print('='*60)",
      "",
      "test_labels = kmeans_final.predict(X_test_scaled)",
      "",
      "X_test_labeled = X_test.copy()",
      "X_test_labeled['Cluster'] = test_labels",
      "",
      "print(f'\\nTest samples per cluster:')",
      "print(X_test_labeled['Cluster'].value_counts().sort_index())",
      "",
      "",
      "# =============================================",
      "# STEP 8: Compare Cluster Distribution (Train vs Test)",
      "# =============================================",
      "print('\\n' + '='*60)",
      "print('STEP 8: CLUSTER CONSISTENCY ANALYSIS')",
      "print('='*60)",
      "",
      "train_dist = X_train_labeled['Cluster'].value_counts(normalize=True).sort_index()",
      "test_dist = X_test_labeled['Cluster'].value_counts(normalize=True).sort_index()",
      "",
      "comparison_df = pd.DataFrame({",
      "    'Train %': (train_dist * 100).round(2),",
      "    'Test %': (test_dist * 100).round(2)",
      "})",
      "comparison_df['Difference'] = (comparison_df['Train %'] - comparison_df['Test %']).abs()",
      "print(comparison_df)",
      "",
      "# Visualize",
      "fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 6))",
      "x = np.arange(len(comparison_df))",
      "width = 0.35",
      "ax.bar(x - width/2, comparison_df['Train %'], width, label='Training', color='steelblue', edgecolor='black')",
      "ax.bar(x + width/2, comparison_df['Test %'], width, label='Testing', color='coral', edgecolor='black')",
      "ax.set_title('Cluster Distribution: Train vs Test', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')",
      "ax.set_xlabel('Cluster')",
      "ax.set_ylabel('Percentage (%)')",
      "ax.set_xticks(x)",
      "ax.set_xticklabels(comparison_df.index)",
      "ax.legend()",
      "ax.grid(axis='y', alpha=0.3)",
      "plt.tight_layout()",
      "plt.show()",
      "",
      "",
      "# =============================================",
      "# STEP 9: PCA Visualization (Train & Test)",
      "# =============================================",
      "print('\\n' + '='*60)",
      "print('STEP 9: PCA VISUALIZATION')",
      "print('='*60)",
      "",
      "# Apply PCA to reduce to 2D",
      "pca = PCA(n_components=2)",
      "X_train_pca = pca.fit_transform(X_train_scaled)",
      "X_test_pca = pca.transform(X_test_scaled)",
      "",
      "print(f'Explained Variance Ratio: {pca.explained_variance_ratio_}')",
      "print(f'Total Variance Explained: {sum(pca.explained_variance_ratio_)*100:.2f}%')",
      "",
      "# Visualize",
      "fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(16, 6))",
      "",
      "# Training clusters",
      "scatter1 = axes[0].scatter(X_train_pca[:, 0], X_train_pca[:, 1], ",
      "                          c=train_labels, cmap='tab10', alpha=0.6, s=50)",
      "axes[0].set_title(f'Training Data Clusters (k={optimal_k})', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')",
      "axes[0].set_xlabel(f'PC1 ({pca.explained_variance_ratio_[0]*100:.1f}%)')",
      "axes[0].set_ylabel(f'PC2 ({pca.explained_variance_ratio_[1]*100:.1f}%)')",
      "plt.colorbar(scatter1, ax=axes[0], label='Cluster')",
      "",
      "# Testing clusters",
      "scatter2 = axes[1].scatter(X_test_pca[:, 0], X_test_pca[:, 1], ",
      "                          c=test_labels, cmap='tab10', alpha=0.6, s=50)",
      "axes[1].set_title(f'Testing Data Clusters (k={optimal_k})', fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')",
      "axes[1].set_xlabel(f'PC1 ({pca.explained_variance_ratio_[0]*100:.1f}%)')",
      "axes[1].set_ylabel(f'PC2 ({pca.explained_variance_ratio_[1]*100:.1f}%)')",
      "plt.colorbar(scatter2, ax=axes[1], label='Cluster')",
      "",
      "plt.tight_layout()",
      "plt.show()",
      "",
      "# Interpretation",
      "print('\\n' + '='*60)",
      "print('INTERPRETATION')",
      "print('='*60)",
      "print(f'\\n1. Optimal clusters identified: k={optimal_k}')",
      "print(f'2. Silhouette score: {max(silhouette_scores):.4f} (higher = better separation)')",
      "print(f'3. PCA explains {sum(pca.explained_variance_ratio_)*100:.1f}% of variance in 2D')",
      "print(f'4. Cluster distribution is {'consistent' if comparison_df[\"Difference\"].max() < 5 else 'somewhat different'} between train and test sets')",
      "print(f'5. Centroids reveal customer segments based on spend, frequency, and recency')",
      "",
      "print('\\n✅ PART 2 COMPLETE: K-Means Clustering Done!')",
      "$0"
    ],
    "description": "Retail Clustering Part 2 - Customer Aggregation, K-Means, Elbow, PCA Visualization"
  }

}
